    Manual/Introduction/Third-Party Libraries

    EximeeBPMS License Book

    Enterprise 1.4.3-ee dependency inventory

    Source: the published 1.4.3-ee aggregate CycloneDX SBOM and the frontend lockfile from tag v1.4.3-ee.

    Java versions and declared licenses come from the published aggregate CycloneDX BOM. It spans engine modules, distributions and development/test modules; the dependencies present in a particular distribution are a subset. JavaScript versions come from the same revision’s frontend lockfile, including build dependencies. Multiple versions can be required by different modules.

    License names below reproduce dependency metadata. Entries without a declaration are marked explicitly. The SPDX license list provides the corresponding standard license texts. For the notices accompanying a particular binary, use its distributed LICENSE and NOTICE files.

names are not used by derivative works. The fonts and derivatives,

or substituting – in part or in whole – any of the components of the
